Seated as claims MS35A-01..14: the levite cities: command the children of israel to give the levites cities to dwell in with pasture-land — a thousand cubits from the wall, two thousand by the cubit on each side, the city in the midst; six cities of refuge and forty-two more, forty-eight in all, from the many more and from the few less; the refuge law: when you cross the jordan you shall appoint cities of refuge for the slayer who smites a soul unwittingly, a refuge from the avenger, that he die not until he stands before the congregation — six cities, three beyond the jordan and three in canaan, for israel, the stranger and the sojourner; the murderer: by an instrument of iron, by a stone of the hand whereby he may die, by a wooden instrument of the hand — he is a murderer, the murderer shall surely die; the avenger of blood puts him to death when he meets him; in hatred thrust, in lying-in-wait thrown, in enmity struck with the hand — the smiter shall surely die; the unwitting: suddenly without enmity he thrust him, or threw any instrument without lying-in-wait, or dropped a stone unseen, and he was not his enemy nor sought his harm — the congregation shall judge between the smiter and the avenger by these judgments; the term: the congregation delivers the slayer from the avenger and returns him to his city of refuge, where he dwells until the death of the high priest anointed with the holy oil; if he goes out beyond its border and the avenger finds him and kills him, he has no blood; after the high priest's death he returns to the land of his possession; a statute of judgment for your generations in all your dwellings: whoever smites a soul, by the mouth of witnesses shall the murderer be slain; one witness shall not testify against a person to die — the daughters' phrase, the blood ban's formula, the sixth and the ninth commandments' verbs in one verse; no ransom for the life of a murderer who is wicked to die — he shall surely die; no ransom for one who fled to his city of refuge, to return to dwell in the land until the death of the priest; you shall not pollute the land, for the blood pollutes the land and no atonement is made for it except by the shedder's blood; you shall not defile the land in whose midst i dwell, for i the lord dwell in the midst of the children of israel — the pollute-root's only torah seat, the ransom's root a third time, the heifer's other passive, the book's inclusio;
